        "description": "Закрытые allowlisted evidence-структуры P1 для каждого checkId. Применимость определяет сервер: агент не передаёт not_applicable. Межполевые и семантические инварианты (freeBytes <= totalBytes, ожидаемые объекты, время) проверяет backend по 05-data-and-api.md и specs/invariants.json."
      },
      "Problem": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "type": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 500
          },
          "title": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 200
          },
          "status": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 400,
            "maximum": 599
          },
          "code": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "invalid_json",
              "unsupported_media_type",
              "payload_too_large",
              "schema_violation",
              "unsupported_schema_version",
              "unknown_check_id",
              "unexpected_subject",
              "duplicate_observation",
              "unknown_policy_version",
              "batch_too_old",
              "batch_time_inconsistent",
              "agent_version_unsupported",
              "idempotency_conflict",
              "sequence_conflict",
              "identity_conflict",
              "organization_inactive",
              "certificate_expired",
              "certificate_revoked",
              "certificate_unknown",
              "renewal_request_conflict",
              "enrollment_token_invalid",
              "installation_already_enrolled",
              "rate_limited",
              "temporarily_unavailable",
              "session_required",
              "csrf_failed",
              "forbidden",
              "not_found",
              "invalid_cursor",
              "revision_mismatch",
              "if_match_required",
              "workflow_transition_forbidden",
              "lifecycle_transition_forbidden",
              "resource_not_ready",
              "validation_failed",
              "policy_inputs_missing"
            ]
          },
          "correlationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "detail": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000
          },
          "instance": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 500
          },
          "minimumAgentVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "type",
          "title",
          "status",
          "code",
          "correlationId"
        ]
      },
      "EnrollmentRequest": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "registrationRequestId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "installationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "displayName": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 120,
            "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F​-‏‪-‮⁦-⁩<>]+$"
          },
          "csrPem": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 8192,
            "pattern": "^-----BEGIN CERTIFICATE REQUEST-----"
          },
          "machineFingerprint": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 64,
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "registrationRequestId",
          "installationId",
          "displayName",
          "csrPem",
          "machineFingerprint"
        ]
      },
      "DeviceCertificate": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "deviceId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,

          },

            "type": "string",

          },

            "type": "string",

            ]

            "enum": [

            "type": [

              "null"
            ],

              "fail",
              "unknown",
              "not_applicable",
              null
            ]
          },
          "freshness": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "fresh",
              "stale"
            ]
          },
          "reasonCode": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "permission_denied",
              "unsupported",
              "collector_timeout",
              "temporary_error",
              "missing_observation",
              "clock_inconsistent",
              "unmapped_state",
              "update_search_failed",
              "update_search_outdated",
              "update_source_mismatch",
              "update_list_truncated",
              "os_version_unknown",
              "os_inventory_missing",
              "applicability_input_missing",
              "policy_not_applicable",
              null
            ]
          },
          "late": {
            "type": "boolean"
          },
          "observedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "receivedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },

            "type": [
              "object",
              "null"
            ]
          },
          "evidenceAvailability": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "available",
              "expired"
            ]
          },
          "explanation": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 2000
          }
        },
        "required": [

          "deviceId",
          "subject",
          "checkId",
          "ruleId",
          "ruleVersion",
          "sourcePolicyVersion",
          "evaluatedPolicyVersion",
          "assessmentScope",
          "assessmentMode",
          "result",
          "freshness",
          "reasonCode",
          "late",
          "observedAt",
          "receivedAt",
          "evidence",
          "evidenceAvailability",
          "explanation"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "assessmentMode": {
                  "const": "informational"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "assessmentMode"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ],

      "FindingEvent": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "occurredAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "eventType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "opened",
              "resolved",
              "reopened",
              "quality_changed",
              "workflow_changed",
              "policy_changed",
              "object_retired"
            ]
          },
          "cause": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "device_change",
              "rule_change",
              "policy_change",
              "data_quality",
              "manual_action",
              "review_expired"
            ]
          },
          "assessmentId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "actorId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "fromWorkflowState": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          },
          "toWorkflowState": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "occurredAt",
          "eventType",
          "cause",
          "assessmentId",
          "actorId",
          "fromWorkflowState",
          "toWorkflowState"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "opened"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "resolved"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "reopened"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "quality_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "data_quality"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "workflow_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "manual_action",
                    "review_expired"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "policy_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "object_retired"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "manual_action"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "const": "manual_action"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "cause"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "actorId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "actorId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

          },
          "dueAt": {
            "type": [
              "string",

            ],

      "RiskDecision": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "decision": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              "withdrawn"
            ]
          },
          "reason": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000
          },
          "reviewAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "decision"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "decision": {
                  "const": "withdrawn"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "decision"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "not": {
                "required": [
                  "reviewAt"
                ]
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "required": [
                "reason",
                "reviewAt"
              ]
            }
          }
        ]

          },

            "enum": [
              "html"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [

          "periodEnd",
          "format"
        ]
      },

        "type": "object",

            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,

Файл `specs/scan-batch.schema.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://contracts.sikkerheit.example.invalid/agent/scan-batch.schema.json",
  "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Scan Batch",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "schemaVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "scanId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
    },
    "sequence": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "agentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
    },
    "policyVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "description": "Версия политики источника."
    },
    "machineFingerprint": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
    },
    "startedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "completedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "scanRequestId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
    },
    "observations": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "observation.schema.json"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 200,
      "description": "Ключ (checkId, subject.type, localKey) уникален внутри пакета; localKey = device для устройства и volumeId для тома. Повтор ключа: 422 duplicate_observation."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "schemaVersion",
    "scanId",
    "sequence",
    "agentVersion",
    "policyVersion",
    "machineFingerprint",
    "startedAt",
    "completedAt",
    "observations"
  ],
  "description": "DeviceId и OrganizationId определяются сертификатом, а не телом пакета. Лимит байтов, idempotency, sequence, окна и время проверяются сервером дополнительно."
}
```
